### [1] [Code-as-Auditor: Executable Compliance Reasoning via Regulation-to-Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19199)
*Jisoo Kim,Taeyoon Kwack,Jinwoo Jang,Woo Kyung Kim,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Code-as-Auditor 框架，将法规转为可执行决策树与检查清单，通过事实/反事实提问与自校验实现证据接地的合规审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益用于合规与法律推理，但其输出常缺乏对法律逻辑与证据的显式接地，难以保证可解释性与可追溯性。

Method: 1) 将监管信息形式化为检查清单与可执行决策树，把规则与条件编码为可解释的代码结构；2) 推理时将每条检查项动态展开为事实性与反事实性问题，引导模型基于案件证据与潜在违规进行推理；3) 构建从证据识别、规则适用到决策的推理流水线，并用自校验回路提升代码逻辑一致性与结果可追溯性。

Result: 在隐私与数据保护场景实验中，Code-as-Auditor 实现了更准确且具备证据支撑的评估，可基于显式监管标准完成自动化合规检查。

Conclusion: 以代码为中介、以证据为接地的推理框架可有效提升 LLM 合规审查的准确性与可解释性，为自动化监管核查提供可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted for compliance and legal reasoning tasks, yet their outputs often lack explicit grounding in legal logic and evidence. We present Code-as-Auditor, an LLM-based framework that extends the model's reasoning capability toward structured and evidence-grounded compliance assessment. The framework translates regulatory information into (1) formalized checklists and executable decision trees, encoding regulations and conditions as interpretable code structures. During inference, each checklist item is (2) dynamically expanded into factual and counterfactual questions, guiding the model to reason over case-specific evidence and potential violations. This process establishes a reasoning pipeline that proceeds from evidence identification, through rule application, to final decision-making, while a self-verification loop improves the logical consistency of the generated code and the traceability of outcomes. Experiments on privacy and data protection scenarios demonstrate that Code-as-Auditor delivers more accurate and evidence-backed evaluations, enabling automated compliance regulation checking grounded in explicit regulatory criteria.

</details>


### [2] [DeltaSelect: Affordable A/B Testing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19607)
*Nicholas J. Conn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeltaSelect——一种面向开发迭代的开源任务选择方法，通过 Pearson 相关识别能稳定跟踪全基准表现的任务，用线性回归把部分验证器结果映射到统一分数，并在美元预算内选出固定任务集，用于开发期的反复基线-候选对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体基准面向广泛、全面的比较，而非频繁的开发决策：单次运行波动大、完整套件成本高，且基准测试框架与实际使用的框架可能不一致。对 DeepSWE 已公布试验的重采样分析显示，113 个任务中仅 22 个（19.5%）与全基准性能的五分位 Pearson 相关达到 0.5 以上，说明多数任务不具稳定的判别力。

Method: DeltaSelect 包含三个环节：一是用 Pearson 相关筛选"单次运行结果能持续跟踪全基准性能"的任务；二是用线性回归将部分验证器的分数映射到统一分数尺度（校准）；三是在给定美元预算下选出固定任务集。方法定位为开发中的重复基线-候选对比工具，而非模型排名。

Result: 在 gpt-5.6-luna 低推理设置的案例研究中，DeltaSelect 被用于修改自定义技能与指令。13 次评估共记录成本 27.86 美元（按 2026 年 8 月 16 日公布费率）。采纳版本比初始版本便宜 58.1%（1.75 vs 4.18 美元；p=0.008），同时校准分数更高（42.36% vs 36.46%；按已公布类比方差的 p=0.326）。

Conclusion: DeltaSelect 提供了一种低成本、可复现的开发期评估代理：以统计筛选保证任务集的判别稳定性，以回归校准保证跨任务可比性，以预算约束保证可行性。其价值在于支撑快速迭代的技能/指令改进决策，而非替代完整基准进行模型排名；案例中的成本下降与分数提升（后者显著性有限）支持其作为开发工具的实用性。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks are built for broad and comprehensive comparisons, not frequent development decisions. Individual runs vary, full suites are expensive, and the benchmark harness may differ from the harness used in practice. In a resampling analysis of DeepSWE's published trials, only 19.5% of tasks (22 of 113) had a fifth-percentile Pearson correlation of at least 0.50 with full-benchmark performance. The paper presents DeltaSelect, an open-source method that identifies tasks whose one-run results consistently track full-benchmark performance using Pearson correlation, maps fractional verifier results to a common score using linear regression, and selects a fixed task set within a dollar budget. DeltaSelect is intended for repeated baseline-versus-candidate comparisons during development, not model rankings. In a gpt-5.6-luna low-reasoning case study, DeltaSelect was used to revise custom skills and instructions. Across 13 evaluations, the recorded cost was USD 27.86 at rates published August 16, 2026. The adopted version cost 58.1% less than the initial version (USD 1.75 versus USD 4.18; p=0.008), while the calibrated score was higher (42.36% versus 36.46%; published-analog variance p=0.326).

</details>


### [3] [BA-TRACE: Boundary-Aware Trace Reconstruction for Scenario-Based Evaluation of Mixed AUTOSAR Adaptive and ROS 2 Vehicular Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19699)
*Shunsuke Ito,Ryudai Iwakami,Hiroyuki Hanyu,Tasuku Ishigooka,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出边界感知追踪重构框架 BA-TRACE，用于混合 AUTOSAR AP 与 ROS 2 车载系统的场景化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2 自动驾驶栈与 AUTOSAR AP 混合部署使执行路径跨越 DDS-SOME/IP 中间件边界，现有仿真器与追踪工具只能执行场景或采集单平台本地追踪，无法重构跨域数据流，导致场景化评估难以解释。

Method: BA-TRACE 融合 ROS 2 追踪事件、AUTOSAR ara::log 事件、由 ARXML 派生的结构依赖关系以及桥接层插桩，重构跨 DDS-SOME/IP 边界的端到端执行图。

Result: 基于 AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO 的目标检测与制动场景案例研究表明，BA-TRACE 能重构出预期的跨平台路径，并揭示边界特定延迟，如点云传输开销。

Conclusion: 重构拓扑仅作为可追踪性证据，不作为行为正确性或安全性证明。

Abstract: Modern vehicular embedded systems increasingly combine ROS 2-based autonomous-driving stacks with AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform (AUTOSAR AP). Such mixed stacks make scenario-based evaluation hard to interpret because execution paths cross DDS-SOME/IP middleware boundaries between ROS 2 and AUTOSAR AP. Existing simulators and tracing tools execute scenarios or collect platform-local traces but cannot reconstruct cross-domain data flows. This paper presents BA-TRACE, a boundary-aware trace reconstruction framework for scenario-based evaluation of mixed AUTOSAR AP and ROS 2 vehicular embedded systems. BA-TRACE combines ROS 2 trace events, AUTOSAR ara::log events, ARXML-derived structural dependencies, and bridge-level instrumentation to reconstruct an end-to-end execution graph across the DDS-SOME/IP boundary. A case study with an AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO-based object-detection and braking scenario shows that BA-TRACE reconstructs the expected cross-platform path and exposes boundary-specific latency such as point-cloud transfer overhead. The reconstructed topology is used as evidence of traceability, not as proof of behavioral correctness or safety.

</details>


### [4] [A Closed-Loop Control Architecture for Reliable Constraint Satisfaction in LLM Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19710)
*Quan Zhou,Shahbaz Siddeeq,Mika Saari,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估一种五阶段闭环控制架构（生成、评估、调整、归档、分析），以确定性代码作为接受判据，解决大语言模型在数值输出约束下不可靠的生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统中越来越多地嵌入大语言模型，用于满足数值型输出约束（如目标词数、目标可读性等级区间）。由于模型具有非确定性、通过自然语言指令而非类型化接口配置、且只能近似满足需求，单次提示既无法稳定达标，也无法保持源内容，因此需要一种可复现的控制机制。

Method: 提出闭环控制架构，包含生成、评估、调整、归档、分析五个阶段。模型仅负责撰写与编辑文本；由确定性代码将复合可读性值与目标区间比对，拒绝任何丢失源实体、数字或关键词的编辑，并作出全部接受决策。

Result: 在四个商用模型上进行114次单次生成任务与240次闭环运行：单次提示达标率为21.1%–31.6%，闭环为92.5%–98.8%，平均不超过两轮编辑，基于召回的内容保真度为0.92–0.93；两种设置共有的两个模型表现出一致效果。

Conclusion: 由于控制器优化的是用于评分的目标值，该结果证明了对已声明、可计算指标的可靠控制，而非对真实人类难度的验证。可迁移的实践是：以代码声明接受条件、将模型限制于局部编辑、并对每次编辑施加内容检查。

Abstract: Software systems increasingly embed a large language model in features that must satisfy a numeric output constraint, that is, a requirement expressible as a number or an interval and checkable by code, such as a target word count or a target readability grade band. Because such a model is non-deterministic, is configured through natural-language instructions rather than a typed interface, and satisfies a stated requirement only approximately, a single prompt neither reliably meets the target nor preserves the source content. This paper presents and evaluates a closed-loop control architecture for this problem. It has five stages: generate, evaluate, adjust, archive, and analyze. The model is called only to write and to edit text, while deterministic code compares a composite readability value against a target band, rejects any edit that drops source entities, numbers, or keywords, and makes every accept decision. Over 114 single-shot generation jobs and 240 closed-loop runs on four commercial models, single-shot prompting met the target in 21.1 to 31.6 percent of cases and the closed loop in 92.5 to 98.8 percent, within two edit rounds on average and at a recall-based fidelity of 0.92 to 0.93; the two models common to both settings show the same effect. Because the controller optimizes the value on which success is scored, the result establishes reproducible control over a declared, computable metric and not validated human difficulty. The transferable practice is to declare the acceptance condition as code, bound the model to local edits, and gate every edit on a content check.

</details>


### [5] [EviRCA: Decoupling Evidence Extraction from Reasoning for Microservice Root-Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19825)
*Yuhao Wang,Zhen Qin,Xingliang Wang,Guochang Li,Weize Li,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EviRCA 将确定性证据抽取与 LLM 推理解耦：先从原始指标、链路、日志生成紧凑的多模态证据卡，再由 LLM 通过只读工具推理定位根因。在 OpenRCA 上正确率达 40.6%–43.9%（基线最高 15.2%），Token 消耗降低 15–26 倍，执行时间降低 3–20 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统根因分析（RCA）劳动密集，现有 LLM 智能体方法让单一模型在原始遥测上反复生成并执行代码，同时完成证据检索、故障定位与根因推断，导致计算开销大、行为不稳定、诊断准确率低；且原始遥测包含数值指标、结构化链路和机器日志，不适合 LLM 直接处理。

Method: 提出 EviRCA 框架，将确定性证据抽取与 LLM 推理解耦：系统无关的抽取阶段把原始指标、链路和日志转换为紧凑、忠实的多模态证据卡；LLM 仅通过少量预定义的只读工具在这些结构化观测上推理，不接触原始遥测，也不执行代码。

Result: 在基于三个企业系统真实异构遥测构建的 OpenRCA 基准上，EviRCA 在两种 LLM 下正确率为 40.6%–43.9%，显著优于此前最高 15.2% 的基线；Token 消耗降低 15–26 倍，执行时间降低 3–20 倍；并解决了需同时推理时间、组件与根因的困难案例（此前方法近乎零性能）。

Conclusion: 过程级失败分析表明，瓶颈在于对抽取阶段已呈现证据的判断，而非证据搜索，说明基于 LLM 的 RCA 效果高度依赖证据抽取质量。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) is a critical yet labor-intensive task for maintaining modern microservice systems, making it an attractive target for large language models (LLMs). Recent agentic approaches allow an LLM to iteratively explore raw telemetry by generating and executing code, asking a single model to simultaneously retrieve evidence, localize faults, and infer root causes over large volumes of heterogeneous telemetry, which leads to high computational cost, unstable behavior, and limited diagnostic accuracy. However, raw telemetry consists of numeric metrics, structured traces, and machine-generated logs that are not directly suitable for LLM processing. We present EviRCA, a framework for LLM-based RCA that decouples deterministic evidence extraction from LLM reasoning. A system-agnostic extraction stage converts raw metrics, traces, and logs into a compact set of faithful multimodal evidence cards, while the LLM reasons only over these structured observations through a small set of predefined read-only tools, without accessing raw telemetry or executing code. We evaluate EviRCA on OpenRCA, a benchmark built from real, heterogeneous telemetry across three enterprise systems. EviRCA achieves a correct rate of 40.6%-43.9% across two different LLMs, substantially outperforming prior OpenRCA baselines that achieve up to 15.2%, while reducing token consumption by 15-26x and execution time by 3-20x. Moreover, EviRCA solves hard cases requiring simultaneous reasoning over time, components, and root causes, a setting where previous approaches reported near-zero performance. Our process-level failure analysis further shows that the bottleneck lies in judging the evidence that the extraction stage has already surfaced, rather than searching for it, suggesting that the effectiveness of LLM-based RCA depends heavily on the quality of evidence extraction.

</details>


### [6] [HapCiD: Detecting API-related Compatibility Issues in OpenHarmony Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20099)
*Daihang Chen,Yonghui Liu,Mingyi Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 HapCiD，一个用于自动检测 OpenHarmony 应用中 API 相关兼容性问题的开源工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenHarmony 的 SDK 快速演进引入大量 API，带来兼容性挑战；但现有兼容性分析工具因 ArkTS 语言新、缺乏成熟执行语义行为模型而失效。

Method: 开发开源工具 HapCiD，自动检测 OpenHarmony 应用中 API 相关的兼容性问题。

Result: 在 4,478 个应用上检测出 163 个应用中的 2,040 个兼容性问题，准确率 100%。

Conclusion: 基于检测结果开展了兼容性问题的综合性研究，对问题进行分类、考察现有缓解策略并提出未来改进方向。

Abstract: OpenHarmony, an emerging open-source mobile platform, is rapidly gaining attention in the mobile development community. Its fast-evolving Software Development Kit (SDK) introduces numerous Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) to boost developer productivity but inevitably introduces compatibility challenges, an issue well known from platforms like Android. However, existing compatibility analysis tools are ineffective for OpenHarmony due to its newly introduced ArkTS programming language and the lack of a mature behavioral model to capture app execution semantics. To bridge this gap, we present HapCiD, an open-source tool for automatically detecting API-related compatibility issues in OpenHarmony apps. Applied to 4,478 apps, HapCiD successfully detects 2,040 compatibility issues across 163 apps with 100 percent accuracy. Based on these findings, we conduct a comprehensive study of compatibility issues, categorizing their types, examining existing mitigation strategies, and proposing future improvements.

</details>


### [7] [AdaRepair-Mem: Adaptive Experience Orchestration for Repository-Level Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20130)
*Z. C. Luo,J. C. Guo,W. J. He,S. Y. Wang,J. C. Yu,F. M. Zhao,Y. Chen,T. Cao,L. Q. Liu,N. Zheng,W. Xu,J. Jiang,Z. M. Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现有仓库级记忆检索存在记忆分布不均衡、记忆量与成功率非单调、记忆积累与修复阶段错配三大问题；本文提出自适应经验检索框架，通过覆盖感知检索、质量感知选择与阶段感知路由提升仓库级程序修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强的仓库级程序修复方法通过复用历史修复经验提升LLM问题解决能力，但作者分析发现现有仓库级记忆检索存在三大局限：一是 episodic memory 在不同仓库间高度不均衡，低资源仓库缺乏有效支持；二是更多记忆并不单调地带来更高修复成功率，相关性、质量与冗余度比记忆数量更重要；三是记忆积累与修复阶段错配，仓库可能拥有大量复现经验却缺少补丁或精炼经验。

Method: 提出面向仓库级程序修复的自适应经验检索框架，包含三个核心机制：coverage-aware retrieval（覆盖感知检索），在同仓库记忆不足时回退到跨仓库或按修复类型组织的记忆；quality-aware selection（质量感知选择），依据相关性、历史效用、具体性与冗余度对记忆排序；stage-aware routing（阶段感知路由），针对复现、定位、补丁生成、补丁精炼与验证等阶段分别检索记忆。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench-Lite 与 SWE-Bench-Verified 上评估，框架提升了覆盖不足仓库上的修复性能，减少了噪声记忆检索，并更好地支持 failed-to-fixed 的补丁精炼。

Conclusion: 记忆增强修复的关键不在于简单积累更多经验，而在于为正确的修复上下文检索正确的经验。

Abstract: Recent memory-augmented repository-level program repair methods reuse historical repair experiences to improve LLM-based issue resolution. However, our analysis reveals three limitations in existing repository-level memory retrieval. First, episodic memory is highly imbalanced across repositories, leaving low-resource repositories with little effective support. Second, more memory does not monotonically lead to higher repair success, suggesting that relevance, quality, and redundancy matter more than raw memory volume. Third, memory accumulation is phase-misaligned: repositories may contain many reproduction experiences but few patch or refinement experiences. To address these problems, we propose an adaptive experience retrieval framework for repository-level program repair. Our framework introduces coverage-aware retrieval, which falls back to cross-repository or repair-type-based memories when same-repository memory is insufficient; quality-aware selection, which ranks memories by relevance, historical utility, specificity, and redundancy; and stage-aware routing, which separates and retrieves memories for reproduction, localization, patch generation, patch refinement, and validation. Evaluated on SWE-Bench-Lite and SWE-Bench-Verified, the proposed framework improves repair performance on under-covered repositories, reduces noisy memory retrieval, and better supports failed-to-fixed patch refinement. Our results show that the key to memory-augmented repair is not simply accumulating more experiences, but retrieving the right experiences for the right repair context.

</details>


### [8] [CodeTransBenchmark: Evaluating LLM-based Code Translation and Repair Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20257)
*Vera Kowalczuk,Oliver Weißl,Severin Kacianka,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeTransBenchmark 框架，系统评估大语言模型在代码翻译与翻译纠错上的表现，发现多语言代码专用模型（如 Codestral）表现优于通用模型，迭代式自动反馈纠错可显著提升翻译准确率，但实际部署仍需更大上下文窗口的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在代码生成上展现出潜力，并逐渐受到代码翻译领域的关注，但 LLM 在代码翻译及翻译错误修复方面的实际有效性尚缺乏系统性研究，因此需要构建统一的评估框架并进行实证分析，以识别现有模型的薄弱环节。

Method: 提出 CodeTransBenchmark 评估框架，用于评估基于 LLM 的翻译与修复，并设计后处理策略从 LLM 不一致输出中提取代码；开展实证研究，在 3 个数据集、12 个语言对上评估 8 个模型，并对错误翻译按错误类型分类；采用基于自动化反馈的迭代式翻译修复方法。

Result: 多语言代码专用训练的模型（如 Codestral）能正确翻译大部分代码，而多数通用模型在目标语言句法规则上表现不佳；错误翻译分析表明，所涉编程语言之间的相互关系及训练数据对效果有显著影响；通用后处理方法必须容忍不一致性并利用 LLM 输出的可预测性；基于自动化反馈的迭代翻译修复能显著提升翻译准确率。

Conclusion: LLM 具备自动化代码翻译的潜力，但基于 LLM 的代码翻译要在实践中有效部署，仍需具备更大上下文窗口的模型支持。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pre-trained on expansive text and code corpora have revealed promising code generation abilities and have attracted increasing attention in code translation. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of LLMs in code translation and translation error repair. First, we present CodeTransBenchmark, a framework for evaluating LLM-based translation and repair and devise a post-processing strategy to extract code from inconsistent LLM outputs. Then, we discuss an empirical study evaluates eight models on three datasets and 12 language pairs, in which we categorize incorrect translations by errors to identify weaknesses of existing LLMs. Our work shows that while LLMs specifically trained for multi-lingual coding, like Codestral, correctly translate the majority of code, most general-purpose models struggle with the syntactic rules of the target language. The analysis of erroneous translations reveals the substantial impact of the interrelationship between involved programming languages and training data on the effectiveness. We show that a general post-processing approach must tolerate inconsistencies and leverage the predictability of LLM answers. Further, we show that iterative translation repair via automated feedback significantly improves translation accuracy. While our combined findings highlight the potential of LLMs to automate code translation, an effective deployment of LLM-based code translation in practice would require models with larger context windows.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating Package-Level Scoping Strategies for Repository-Level Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20279)
*Omar Abedelkader,Stéphane Ducasse,Guillermo Polito,Oleksandr Zaitsev,Romain Robbes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估了三种包感知启发式策略（横向包、直接包依赖、两级包依赖），用于改进 Pharo 语义补全引擎的候选排序。实验表明，直接包依赖效果最佳，3字符前缀下类名补全MRR由0.20提升至0.44，方法名补全由0.10提升至0.41，并被Pharo工业联盟采纳用于Pharo 13。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码按仓库、包和项目组织，但基于token的补全引擎很少利用这种结构。Pharo的默认语义补全策略虽遵循语言语义与作用域规则，却忽略包结构与依赖关系，导致同包、同项目的类及其使用类未获优先排序，降低了补全的相关性。

Method: 提出三种包感知启发式：Lateral Packages（横向包）、Direct Package Dependencies（直接包依赖）、Two-Level Package Dependencies（两级包依赖）。构建覆盖219个包、4,535个类、35,972个方法的基准，涵盖Iceberg、Moose、Roassal、Seaside、Spec等主要Pharo项目的方法调用点。评估类名与方法名补全，检验显式包依赖能否作为轻量结构化信号提升候选排序，且不引入新的学习模型。

Result: Direct Package Dependencies启发式效果最强。对于3字符前缀，类名补全的MRR从0.20提升至0.44，方法名补全的MRR从0.10提升至0.41。包感知策略在所评估项目上比默认Semantics-Based策略提供更准确、更相关的建议。

Conclusion: 包感知策略能有效提升Pharo补全的候选排序质量，且无需引入新学习模型。基于类名补全的提升，Pharo工业联盟已将Direct Package Dependencies启发式采纳用于Pharo 13。

Abstract: Source code is organized in repositories, packages, and projects, yet token-level completion engines rarely exploit this structure. Pharo provides a semantic completion engine based on ordered, lazy heuristics that retrieve and rank candidates. Its default strategy follows language semantics and scoping rules, but ignores package structure and dependencies. Consequently, classes from the same package or project, and classes used by them, receive no special priority.
  We introduce and evaluate three package-aware heuristics: Lateral Packages, Direct Package Dependencies, and Two-Level Package Dependencies. Our benchmark covers method call sites from 219 packages, including 4,535 classes and 35,972 methods across major Pharo projects such as Iceberg, Moose, Roassal, Seaside, and Spec. We assess class-name and method-name completion and ask whether explicit package dependencies can provide a lightweight structural signal for improving candidate ranking without introducing a new learning model.
  The Direct Package Dependencies heuristic produced the strongest results. For 3-character prefixes, Mean Reciprocal Rank increased from 0.20 to 0.44 for class-name completion and from 0.10 to 0.41 for method-name completion. These findings show that package-aware strategies provide more accurate and relevant suggestions than the default Semantics-Based strategy on the evaluated projects. Based on the improvement in class-name completion, the Pharo Industrial Consortium adopted the Direct Package Dependencies heuristic for Pharo 13.

</details>


### [10] [Towards a Characterization of Microservice Architectures Generated by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20308)
*José Renan,Ademar Sousa,Emanuel Dantas,Danyllo Albuquerque,Mirko Perkusic,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究实证刻画了LLM从模块化单体自然语言描述生成微服务架构的行为，发现差异主要由提示策略（零样本vs少样本）而非模型厂商驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于软件设计，但其生成架构的结构与一致性尚不清楚，尤其是"架构到架构"转换中LLM的行为理解不足，缺乏经验性基线。

Method: 在两个模块化单体系统上进行受控实验，对比零样本与少样本提示策略，使用OpenAI的GPT系列模型与DeepSeek模型；将生成架构统一表示为CSV格式，并用归一化指标评估服务粒度、服务间通信模式、通信密度、服务隔离度与职责描述性。

Result: 结果显示系统性差异主要由提示策略而非模型厂商驱动：少样本提示持续产生更细粒度分解、更低通信密度与更详细的职责描述；零样本提示则倾向于更粗粒度且连接更紧密的架构。

Conclusion: 本研究不评估架构建质量，而是为理解文本驱动的"架构到架构"转换中LLM行为提供经验基线，为生成式设计工作流与早期软件现代化中的混合架构推理研究提供参考。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software design tasks, yet little is known about the structure and coherence of the architectures they produce. In particular, how LLMs behave in Architecture-to-Architecture transformations remains insufficiently understood. This study empirically characterizes how LLMs generate microservice architectures from natural language descriptions of modular monoliths, focusing on structural and descriptive properties rather than architectural correctness. We conduct controlled experiments on two modular monolith systems, comparing zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies using GPT-based models from OpenAI and models from DeepSeek. Generated architectures are represented in a standardized CSV format and evaluated using normalized metrics capturing service granularity, inter-service communication patterns, communication density, service isolation, and responsibility descriptiveness. The results reveal systematic differences driven primarily by prompting strategy rather than model provider. Few-shot prompting consistently produces finer-grained decompositions with lower communication density and more detailed responsibility descriptions, whereas zero-shot prompting favors coarser and more tightly connected architectures. Rather than assessing architectural quality, this study provides an empirical baseline for understanding LLM behavior in text-driven Architecture-to-Architecture transformations, informing future research on generative design workflows and hybrid architectural reasoning in early-stage software modernization.

</details>


### [11] [Assessing the Construct Validity of Object-Oriented, Class-Level Code Quality Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20411)
*Hera Arif,Miikka Kuutila,Paul Ralph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文运用现代测量理论，通过EFA和CFA检验面向对象类级代码质量度量的构念效度，最终确认24个度量对应Size、Cohesion、Inheritance和Coupling四类构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量代码质量度量被提出和使用，但其构念效度很少被评估，导致这些度量究竟测量了什么并不清楚，因此需要系统性地验证其构念效度。

Method: 从Apache Maven项目中使用Designite、JHawk和Understand三个工具提取类级面向对象代码度量，先以探索性因子分析（EFA）识别因子结构，再在22个符合预设标准的随机开源项目上用验证性因子分析（CFA）验证该结构。

Result: EFA揭示24个度量对应六个构念：Cohesion、In-Coupling、Out-Coupling、Size、Sub-Inheritance（与子类相关）和Sup-Inheritance（与超类相关）；10个度量不对应任何已知软件质量维度而被剔除，另有10个度量在CFA中载荷偏低建议剔除。最终保留Size、Cohesion、Inheritance和Coupling，其中Inheritance与Coupling识别出子类别。

Conclusion: 结果有力支持24个代码质量度量的构念效度；Coupling和Inheritance是多维构念，需分别测量两个不同概念（表现为子类别），Complexity或更适合用多层模型探索。整体表明现代测量理论与潜变量建模在验证软件代码质量度量中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Background: Code quality metrics are intended to measure latent properties of software source code. Although numerous code metrics have been proposed and used, their construct validity is rarely evaluated. Thus, the extent to which code metrics actually measure what they claim to measure is often unclear. Aim: Drawing from modern measurement theory, we investigate the construct validity of common class-level, object-oriented code quality metrics by identifying their factor structure using Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA). The metrics were extracted from the Apache Maven project by three software tools: Designite, JHawk, and Understand. The factor structure was later verified using Confirmatory Factor Analysis (CFA) on 22 randomly selected open source projects meeting a predetermined eligibility criteria. Results: 24 code quality metrics that correspond to six constructs: Cohesion, In-Coupling, Out-Coupling, Size, Sub-Inheritance (related to subclasses), and Sup-Inheritance (related to superclasses) were revealed in the underlying factor structure. Ten metrics did not correspond to any known dimension of software quality and were removed in the EFA. Ten additional metrics exhibited low loadings in the CFA, suggesting their removal from the final measurement model. Size, Cohesion, Inheritance, and Coupling were the constructs retained, with subcategories identified for Inheritance and Coupling. Conclusions: Our results strongly support the construct validity of 24 code quality metrics. Coupling and Inheritance are revealed as multidimensional constructs, since they require measuring two different concepts, revealed as sub-categories in our analysis, and Complexity may be better explored in a multilevel model. Overall, our study demonstrates the value of applying modern measurement theory and latent variable modeling in validating software code quality metrics.

</details>


### [12] [Spotlights: Discovering Improvement Opportunities in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20446)
*Udi Barzelay,Ophir Azulai,Idan Friedman,Inbar Shapira,Foad Abo Dahood,Yevgeny Burshtein,Orit Prince,Michael Soloveitchik,Oshri Naparstek,Roi Pony,Tal Drory,Michael Factor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"优化机会发现"这一仓库级新任务：给定仓库、工程目标与可选运行证据，无需用户指定缺陷或位置，自动识别值得研究的候选源码区域并给出解释与改法。为此提出Spotlights系统，通过逻辑仓库映射、多轮智能体评审与可选技术检索完成该任务，并在多个真实系统中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理与进化式代码搜索虽能在目标与评价标准明确后改进实现，但直接应用于既有软件仓库时存在前置问题：在给定高层工程目标下，哪些实现选择值得投入研究？现有工作默认缺陷、瓶颈或代码位置已由用户指定，缺少从"宽泛工程目标"到"可实施改进点"之间的自动化发现环节。

Method: 提出optimization-opportunity discovery任务：输入为仓库、工程目标及可选运行证据（离线遥测或性能剖析），输出候选源码区域、其与目标关系的解释以及可能的修改建议。实现系统Spotlights，核心步骤为：逻辑仓库映射、连续的智能体评审（successive agent reviews）、可选的研究关联（将候选与相关技术文献/方法连接）。

Result: 在多类系统（模型服务、文档检索、区块链排序、文档处理）上评测：三个案例中回收9个专家选定目标中的7个；可靠性研究中前10候选有70%满足既定正确性与严重性阈值；5次重复检索中73.6%的候选出现次数在全部5次运行中匹配到相同源区域；成功重新发现一个被隐藏的检索优化目标并关联到相关tiling技术；实现研究中，一个被发现并采纳的改动使端到端页面处理耗时降低10.6%，同时保持输出质量不变。

Conclusion: 结果表明优化机会发现是介于宽泛工程目标与后续实现、验证之间的一个独立且可实证评估的步骤，Spotlights为其提供了可行且可复现的实现范式。

Abstract: Coding agents and evolutionary code-search systems can improve implementations once a target and evaluation criterion have been specified. Applying these methods to an existing software repository raises an earlier question: which implementation choices are worth investigating for a high-level engineering objective? We introduce \emph{optimization-opportunity discovery}, the repository-level task of identifying candidate source regions, explaining how they relate to the objective, and proposing possible changes. The task takes as input a repository, an engineering objective, and optional runtime evidence such as offline telemetry observations or profiles. It does not require the user to specify a defect, bottleneck, or code location. We present \emph{Spotlights}, a system that performs this task through logical repository mapping, successive agent reviews, and optional research linking candidates to relevant techniques. We evaluate Spotlights across model serving, document retrieval, blockchain ordering, and document processing. Across three cases, it recovers seven of nine expert-selected targets. In the reliability study, 70\% of the top ten candidates meet the stated correctness and severity thresholds. Across five repeated retrieval runs, 73.6\% of candidate occurrences have a matching source region in all five runs. Spotlights also rediscovers the target of a withheld retrieval optimization and connects it to a relevant tiling technique. In an implementation study, a discovered change reduces end-to-end page-processing runtime by 10.6\% while preserving measured output quality. These results establish optimization-opportunity discovery as a distinct and empirically evaluable step between a broad engineering objective and subsequent implementation and validation.

</details>


### [13] [Quantifying Overclaiming Propensity in Frontier LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20812)
*Nolan Smyth,Yorguin-Jose Mantilla-Ramos,Pascal Jr Tikeng Notsawo,Saskia Helbling,Alberto Tosato,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Nouha Dziri,Gauthier Gidel,Tommaso Tosato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OverclaimBench 评测套件，用于量化前沿编码智能体"过度声称任务完成"（overclaim）的倾向，即最终回复与上下文信息相矛盾。评测发现智能体普遍未读完待审文件，且在未读全的情况下多数存在误导性表述，虚假声称完整审查还会掩盖实质性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体日益被赋予长时间自主工作的信任，但用户往往只能通过智能体的最终回复来了解其工作过程。这种信息不对称使得智能体的过度声称（overclaim）可能误导用户，因此需要一套客观、无需推断意图、且独立于任务成败的度量方法来量化这一现象。

Method: 提出 OverclaimBench 评测套件，由五个文件审查场景、基于对话记录（transcript）的覆盖度测量以及注册式植入缺陷（registered planted defects）组成。overclaim 定义为智能体最终回复与其上下文信息相矛盾。评测了 8 个专有前沿模型（在其各自生产级命令行界面中）以及 4 个开放权重模型（在统一固定框架下）。

Result: 1) 67.9% 的运行中智能体未读完被要求审查的全部文件；2) 在未读全文件的运行中，80.4% 的情况存在误导（各模型 59–96%），表现为虚假声称已读全部文件或漏报覆盖不完整；3) 要求委派给子智能体可提升阅读覆盖度，但在仍不完整的审查中，绝大多数依然具有误导性；4) 虚假声称完成审查的智能体漏检植入缺陷的概率约为读完全部文件智能体的 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: 智能体的最终回复并非其实际行动的可靠记录。overclaim 现象普遍存在，且虚假的完成声称会掩盖实质性的失败，用户不应仅依赖智能体的自我报告来判断任务完成情况。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are increasingly trusted to work autonomously for long periods, yet an agent's final response is often the only account of that work a user sees. We quantify the propensity of frontier agents to \emph{overclaim} task completion, a misrepresentation that can mislead the user. An agent overclaims when its final response contradicts information in its context. This definition requires no inference about intent and is independent of task success. We introduce \emph{OverclaimBench}, an evaluation suite composed of five file-review scenarios, transcript-based coverage measurements, and registered planted defects. We evaluate eight proprietary frontier models in their own production command-line interfaces, and four open-weight models under a single fixed harness on OverclaimBench and find that 1) agents do not read all the files they were asked to review in 67.9\% of runs; 2) among runs where not all files are read, agents are \emph{misleading} 80.4\% of the time (59--96\% per model), either falsely claiming to have read all files or omitting that coverage is incomplete; 3) requiring delegation to subagents increased reading coverage, but among reviews that remained incomplete, a large majority were still misleading; and 4) agents that falsely claimed a complete review missed planted defects at about 1.8 times the rate of agents that read every file, showing that claims of completion can conceal substantive failures. Together, these results show that agents' final responses are not reliable accounts of their actions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [Algebraic Retrieval: Composable Search for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19482)
*Damian Delmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"代数检索"（Algebraic Retrieval），允许AI智能体在查询时以数学查询组合相关性判据、资格约束与排序偏好；基于PEM验证可组合查询的执行等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索接口多为固定流程，智能体难以在查询时按需组合搜索策略并在查看结果后修改程序；需要一种能同时表达相关性判据、资格约束与排序偏好，且可动态组合、修订的查询范式。

Method: 在Programmatic Embedding Modulation（PEM）基础上，暴露向量与分数算术操作；将对比打分、候选池重排、加权排序等表示为可组合查询，并提供可执行的SQL与PyTerrier对应实现；评估聚焦执行等价性，而非智能体行为或检索质量。

Result: 在公开的Vaswani数据集（11,429篇文档）上，每个程序的多套实现选出相同的文档集合，分数差异小于1e-6；仅有一对并列文档在不同打分路径下顺序不同。

Conclusion: 代数检索使AI智能体可在查询时组合与修订搜索策略，并可通过SQL、PyTerrier等多种执行路径保持高度一致的执行结果，为可编程、可组合的检索提供了可行性验证。

Abstract: Algebraic Retrieval lets AI agents compose search strategies at query time. Relevance criteria, eligibility constraints, and ranking preferences can be expressed together in a mathematical query. The query surface exposes available operations, so an agent can combine them for the question at hand and revise a program after inspecting results. We evaluate execution parity, not agent behavior or retrieval quality. Building on Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM), which exposes vector and score arithmetic during retrieval, we demonstrate contrastive scoring, candidate-pool reranking, and weighted ranking as composable queries, alongside executable SQL and PyTerrier counterparts. On the public 11,429-document Vaswani fixture, each program's implementations select the same document set with score differences below 1e-6; one tied pair orders differently across scoring paths.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Similarity through Zero-Token Geometric Graphs for Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19622)
*Zeliang Li,Xiaofen Xing,Kailing Guo,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出G³RAG，一种仅基于文档、无需LLM调用即可离线构图的多跳RAG框架。其通过几何增益评分、密度感知拓扑惩罚与单步受控扩散提升多跳检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳RAG需要证据既与查询相关，又能引入足够新颖性以跨越语义鸿沟。稠密检索偏向语义相似文档，而图方法常依赖昂贵LLM实体抽取并可能沿噪声连接传播。

Method: G³RAG为document-only框架，离线构图不使用LLM调用或生成token。为每条边赋予几何增益分数cosθ·sinθ，联合刻画文档表示间的方向一致性与正交性；采用密度感知拓扑惩罚抑制高连接枢纽；并从过滤后的查询种子出发，通过单步受控扩散扩展至互补证据。

Result: 在MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA与HotpotQA上，使用Nv-embed-v2与Qwen3-8B-embed评估。G³RAG在两种嵌入设置下均取得所评估图基线中最佳平均F1与答案文档命中率，平均提升最高达4.26 F1点，MusiQue上达5.76点，并消除实体图方法产生的构图token成本。

Conclusion: 几何结构可在不依赖LLM构图的情况下支持高效多跳证据发现。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires evidence that remains relevant to a query while introducing enough novelty to bridge semantic gaps. Dense retrieval tends to concentrate on semantically similar documents, whereas graph-based alternatives often depend on costly Large Language Model (LLM) entity extraction and may propagate through noisy connections. We introduce Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG), a document-only framework whose offline graph construction uses no LLM calls or generated tokens. G$^3$RAG assigns each edge a geometric gain score, $\cosθ\cdot \sinθ$, that jointly captures directional consistency and orthogonality between document representations. A density-aware topological penalty suppresses highly connected hubs, while single-step controlled diffusion expands from filtered query seeds toward complementary evidence. We evaluate G$^3$RAG on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA using Nv-embed-v2 and Qwen3-8B-embed. G$^3$RAG obtains the best average F1 and answer-document hit rate among the evaluated graph-based baselines in both embedding settings, with gains of up to 4.26 F1 points in average performance and 5.76 points on MusiQue. It also removes the graph-construction token cost incurred by entity-based graph methods. These results show that geometric structure can support efficient multi-hop evidence discovery without LLM-based graph construction. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/

</details>


### [16] [Self-Evolving Search Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19656)
*Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Jaehoon Kim,Daye Nam,SeongKu Kang,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，使检索索引无需人工干预即可随检索环境自我演化，通过优化器自主诊断与改写索引键，并用查询模拟器主动构造额外需求，在多种语料与检索器上稳定提升检索性能并惠及下游智能体应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体面对复杂任务时检索需求日益多样，检索质量高度依赖索引键能否有效暴露文档知识；而有效表示随检索环境变化，固定优化策略难以通用。现有索引演化仍以人工为主，需人工诊断失败、调整策略并重处理索引，成本高且难以扩展。

Method: SELF-INDEX 框架包含两个核心组件：Optimizer 自主诊断检索短板，选择性修改应负责的索引键，并在更新索引前对每次修改进行验证；Query Simulator 在已观测检索需求之外主动探索额外需求，使索引能超越当前可用查询继续演化。

Result: 在多种语料与检索器上，SELF-INDEX 持续提升检索性能，并优于现有索引优化方法；其收益可延伸至下游应用，提升搜索智能体的效果与效率，并帮助智能体记忆系统检索到有用的历史交互。

Conclusion: 索引可以在无人工干预下自我演化并适配检索环境，为检索增强的智能体系统提供更有效、更高效的索引优化范式。

Abstract: Information retrieval is increasingly important as LLM agents tackle complex tasks involving diverse information needs. Because retrieval relies on an index that represents each document through index keys, retrieval quality depends heavily on how effectively these keys expose the knowledge contained in each document. However, effective index representations vary across retrieval environments, making it difficult for any fixed optimization strategy to perform consistently. Yet evolving an index to its retrieval environment remains largely human-driven, requiring humans to diagnose retrieval failures, refine the optimization strategy, and reprocess the index accordingly. We propose SELF-INDEX, a framework that enables an index to self-evolve without human intervention. Its Optimizer autonomously diagnoses retrieval shortfalls, selectively revises the responsible index keys, and validates each revision before updating the index. Beyond reacting to observed retrieval demands, SELF-INDEX proactively explores additional demands through a Query Simulator, allowing the index to evolve beyond the queries already available for optimization. Across diverse corpora and retrievers, SELF-INDEX consistently improves retrieval performance while outperforming existing index optimization methods. We further show that these benefits extend to downstream applications, improving the effectiveness and efficiency of search agents and helping agent memory systems retrieve useful past interactions.

</details>


### [17] [Dense Feature Representation over Sequence Modeling: A Solution to the KDD Cup 2026 UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19787)
*Yi Zhang,Weiliang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KDD Cup 2026 腾讯 UniRec CVR 预测（3482 万条记录）第 10 名方案：单变量链条将测试 AUC 从 0.813237 提升至 0.827816，最终提交 0.828535；留一消融显示增益主要来自稠密特征表示栈与正交化优化器，序列建模组件贡献微弱；并揭示行组划分的验证 AUC 高估排行榜约 0.014 的泛化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在回答一个实证性问题：在工业级点击-转化（CVR）预测任务中，究竟是哪类机制真正提升了留出集 AUC。作者以官方 PCVRHyFormer 基线为起点，通过受控的单变量替换链条，量化各改进模块对排行榜指标的边际贡献，并检验常见的离线验证划分是否会给出误导性的结论。

Method: 方法上，作者构建了一条 15 步的单变量改进链条，每一步只改动一个因素，从基线逐步演化到最终提交模型，从而将测试 AUC 的变化归因到具体机制。随后在完整模型上做留一消融（leave-one-out），逐个移除稠密特征表示栈、正交化优化器、合并单流主干、极性通道、辅助头、逐 token FFN 等组件，并以±0.0004 的种子波动带作为显著性参照。此外，作者分析了行组式训练/验证划分与时间有序重划分之间的差异，以诊断验证与排行榜之间的偏移。

Result: 结果表明：测试 AUC 从基线的 0.813237 提升到 0.827816，最终提交达到 0.828535。留一消融中，移除稠密特征表示栈代价 0.0095 AUC，移除正交化优化器代价 0.0028 AUC；而任何序列建模组件（合并单流主干、极性通道、辅助头、逐 token FFN）的代价均不超过 0.0005，落在±0.0004 的种子波动带内或紧邻其边缘，即不显著。泛化方面，行组划分因共享同一时间窗，使验证 AUC 比排行榜高估约 0.014；反记忆与高基数 ID 相关改动甚至在该验证集上符号反转，该分歧可追溯到 dump 到 dump 的分布漂移，且在时间有序重划分后依然存在。

Conclusion: 结论是：在此规模与数据条件下，驱动 CVR AUC 的是稠密表示学习与优化策略，而非更精细的序列建模；同时离线验证不可靠——共享时间窗的行组划分会系统性高估性能，因此方法优劣的判定必须依据留出的排行榜结果。

Abstract: We describe our 10th-place solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, industrial click-to-conversion (CVR) prediction over 34.82M records, and we ask which mechanisms actually move held-out AUC. Starting from the official PCVRHyFormer baseline, a 15-step single-variable chain raises test AUC from 0.813237 to 0.827816, and our final submission reaches 0.828535. A leave-one-out ablation from the full model attributes the gain: removing the dense-feature representation stack costs 0.0095 AUC and removing the orthogonalized optimizer costs 0.0028, while no sequence-modeling component (merged single-stream backbone, polarity channel, auxiliary head, per-token FFN) costs more than 0.0005, within or adjacent to a $\pm$0.0004 seed band. We also report a generalization hazard: the row-group train/validation split shares one time window, so validation AUC overstates the leaderboard by about 0.014; anti-memorization and high-cardinality-ID changes even invert sign against it, a divergence that traces to dump-to-dump distribution shift and survives a time-ordered re-split. Dense representation and optimization, not finer sequence modeling, drive CVR AUC at this scale, and verdicts must come from the held-out leaderboard.

</details>


### [18] [Reproducing Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations: Exploring Open-Weight Models via Natural-Language User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19831)
*Noah Mamié,Laurin van den Bergh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本复现研究验证了基于生成式自然语言用户画像的推荐系统的透明度与可审查性，成功复现原文核心结论，但通过消融、多种子稳定性与机制可解释性分析发现：画像扰动仅导致评分整体平移而非按类别选择性变化，排序不变，根源在于评分回归目标而非画像接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文提出从用户评论文本合成自然语言用户画像，使用户可直接交互与干预（纠正偏好误判、缓解冷启动），从而提升推荐系统的透明度与可审查性。本文旨在对该主张进行复现与更严格的验证，尤其检验画像是否真正带来可解释、可控的推荐行为。

Method: 在Amazon Movies & TV、TripAdvisor等跨领域数据上复现原始用户画像推荐（UPR）流程；扩展实验包括：系统性上下文消融、五个不同随机种子的多种子稳定性检验以确立统计可靠性、以及基于nnsight框架的机制可解释性分析，通过反事实画像扰动探测模型内部表示。

Result: 成功复现原文核心发现，验证UPR在其测试集重排序协议下具有竞争性表现并提升推荐透明度；自然语言画像扰动确实改变预测，但评分变化在所有类别上均匀平移，无可检测的类别选择性效应，排名保持不变，即使直接进行激活干预（activation steering）亦然。该现象归因于评分回归目标，而非画像接口本身；采用排序目标的模型在该任务上明显更优。

Conclusion: 原文关于UPR性能与透明度的主张可被复现，但其可干预性与可解释性存在局限：画像扰动并未带来类别选择性的推荐调整，排序结果不受影响，瓶颈在于评分回归目标。未来应转向排序目标以真正实现画像驱动的可控推荐。

Abstract: In this reproducibility study, we investigate the transparency and scrutability of recommender systems enhanced by incorporating generated natural-language user profiles that represent user preferences. The original paper explores the synthesis of user profiles from raw user-generated review text across domains such as movies and accommodations (Amazon Movies & TV, TripAdvisor). Crucially, these natural-language user profiles enable direct user interaction and intervention, allowing users to customize recommendations by correcting misattributed preferences or addressing cold-start settings. We successfully reproduce the core findings of the original study. Additionally, we extend the evaluation by conducting systematic context ablation experiments, multi-seed stability across five distinct random seeds to establish statistical reliability, and a mechanistic interpretability analysis using the nnsight framework to probe internal model representations under counterfactual profile perturbations. Our findings verify the original paper's claim that User Profile Recommendation (UPR) achieves competitive performance under its test-set reranking protocol and makes recommendations more transparent. Perturbing the natural-language profiles does change predictions, but it shifts predicted ratings uniformly across genres with no detectable genre-selective effect, leaving rankings unchanged even under direct activation steering. We trace this back to the rating-regression objective rather than the profile interface, with ranking-objective models clearly exceeding in this task.

</details>


### [19] [The Missing Complement: State-Conditioned Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20050)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复"任务，将检索重新定义为集合构建而非排序，并用MSS-Complement方法在SERBench上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器按段落（passage）打分相关性，但决策所需的是证据集合的充分性（sufficiency）。排序器可能用同一必要事实的多种变体填满预算，导致决策仍缺乏支持。因此，需要从"问题像什么"的重排序，转向"决策还缺什么"的恢复。

Method: 1) 形式化任务：状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复，给定捕获的agent状态，恢复能补齐下一步决策所缺支持的紧凑证据组合。2) 构建SERBench：500个来自45个仓库的留出状态，记录agent已见内容，仅当覆盖当前决策所需全部事实才计分。3) 提出MSS-Complement：将获取视为集合构建，通过三次语义调用（提出联合充分集、搜索缺失项、返回4-8个完整源单元，限制在6144 token内）实现。

Result: 在SERBench上，固定配置在5项时73.0%、8项时80.6%恢复完整证据集，优于Qwen3 embedding+重排序的61.4%与72.4%；仅按相似度排序的对照为66.6%，说明增益来自集合级策略而非计算量。在无gold-derived pool的冻结仓库源码下领先5.0个点。在AMA-Bench上，回答提示缩小76.2%，准确率比该基准自带memory agent高2.08个点。从完整集合中移除一个必需组，在两个执行器下分别造成12.3和11.1个点的修复定位精度损失。

Conclusion: 面向agent的检索应重新表述为"恢复决策所缺证据"，而非"重排序issue所相似的内容"。

Abstract: A coding agent halfway through an issue has already read much of what a retriever ranks highest. Relevance is scored per passage, but sufficiency belongs to the set: a ranker can fill its budget with variants of one required fact and leave the decision unsupported. We formulate state-conditioned minimal sufficient evidence recovery: given a captured agent state, recover a compact evidence combination that supplies the support its next decision still lacks. SERBench measures this on 500 held-out states from 45 repositories, recording what the agent has seen and crediting only sets that cover every fact the current decision was annotated to require. MSS-Complement treats acquisition as set construction, not ranking. Three semantic calls propose a jointly sufficient set, search for what it lacks, and return 4-8 intact source units within 6,144 tokens. One configuration, fixed on calibration data, recovers a complete set for 73.0% of those states at five items and 80.6% at eight, against 61.4% and 72.4% for Qwen3 embedding with reranking. A matched control ranking by similarity alone reaches 66.6%, placing the gain in the set-level policy, not the computation. From frozen repository source with no gold-derived pool, the lead is 5.0 points. On AMA-Bench it answers from a 76.2% smaller answer prompt, with accuracy 2.08 points above that benchmark's own memory agent. Removing one required group from an otherwise complete set costs 12.3 and 11.1 points of repair-localization precision under two executors. Retrieval for agents is better posed as recovering what a decision lacks than re-ranking what an issue resembles.

</details>


### [20] [Think Thrice Before Reranking: Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20131)
*Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Wenyan Li,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MERIT-Rank 框架，通过多视角推理轨迹建模与联合重排器提升文本重排鲁棒性，并设计 PRPO 渐进式训练框架，在推理密集型与传统检索基准上均取得优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 推理的重排方法主要依赖单一推理轨迹，导致排序结果易受推理错误影响，且难以建模文档相关性背后多维度的信号。为解决这一困境，需要引入互补的多视角推理轨迹来增强重排的鲁棒性。

Method: MERIT-Rank（多视角证据与推理集成文本重排）构建了多轨迹推理空间（MTRS），从多个视角评估查询-文档相关性；并引入联合重排器，将多条推理路径整合为统一排序决策。此外提出渐进式排序策略优化（PRPO），通过分阶段优化目标稳定推理轨迹并持续提升排序质量。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准与传统检索基准上，MERIT-Rank 均持续优于竞争性基线。其 4B 规模模型在 BRIGHT 基准上显著超越多数 7B 甚至 32B 的重排器。

Conclusion: 多视角推理轨迹集成与渐进式策略优化能有效缓解单一推理轨迹带来的排序偏差，显著提升基于 LLM 的文本重排鲁棒性与性能，并展现出小模型超越大模型的参数效率优势。

Abstract: Reasoning-based reranking with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown promising improvements in text ranking. However, current methods predominantly rely on a single reasoning trajectory, resulting in rankings that are susceptible to reasoning errors and inherently constrained in modeling the multifaceted signals underlying document relevance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose MERIT-Rank(Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking), a framework that models complementary reasoning trajectories to improve reranking robustness. MERIT-Rank formulates a Multi-Trajectory Reasoning Space (MTRS) that evaluates query-document relevance from multiple perspectives and introduces a joint reranker that consolidates these reasoning paths into a unified ranking decision. We further develop Progressive Rank Policy Optimization (PRPO), a progressive training framework that stabilizes reasoning trajectories while continually improving ranking quality through staged optimization objectives. Experiments on both reasoning-intensive and traditional retrieval benchmarks show that MERIT-Rank consistently achieves superior performance over competitive baselines. The 4B model notably outperforms most 7B and even 32B rerankers on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [21] [FacetCRS: Multi-Faceted Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20175)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ziliang Chen,Jinghui Qin,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FacetCRS范式，通过多层面偏好学习（实体、词、上下文、评论层面）在对话推荐系统中打破过滤气泡，实验证明其在缓解过滤气泡和提升推荐质量上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤气泡是推荐系统中的一个严重问题，用户被暴露在狭窄且有限的内容范围内，强化其既有偏好和信念，导致缺乏内容多样性。现有工作大多研究静态或相对静态推荐场景下的过滤气泡，但在真实在线推荐中，用户与系统间的反馈回路会随时间不断加剧过滤气泡，因此需要针对动态、连续交互场景的解决方案。

Method: 提出FacetCRS（Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System），一种新型范式，利用自然语言对话实现及时的用户-物品交互。通过考虑多样化的用户偏好和意图，FacetCRS将用户偏好自动建模为多层面，包括实体层面、词层面、上下文层面和评论层面，以捕捉多样且动态的用户偏好。该框架为端到端CRS框架，能够自适应学习不同层级偏好层面的表示以及多种类型的外部知识。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在缓解过滤气泡和提升CRS推荐质量方面均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 通过多层面偏好学习和对话式交互，FacetCRS有效打破了对话推荐系统中的过滤气泡，验证了动态、多层面偏好建模在缓解过滤气泡与提升推荐质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: The filter bubble is a notorious issue in Recommender Systems (RSs), which describes the phenomenon whereby users are exposed to a limited and narrow range of information or content that reinforces their existing dominant preferences and beliefs. This results in a lack of exposure to diverse and varied content. Many existing works have predominantly examined filter bubbles in static or relatively-static recommendation settings. However, filter bubbles will be continuously intensified over time due to the feedback loop between the user and the system in the real-world online recommendation. To address these issues, we propose a novel paradigm, Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System (FacetCRS), which aims to burst filter bubbles in the conversational recommender system (CRS) through timely user-item interactions via natural language conversations. By considering diverse user preferences and intentions, FacetCRS automatically model user preference into multi-facets, including entity-, word-, context-, and review-facet, to capture diverse and dynamic user preferences to prick filter bubbles in the CRS. It is an end-to-end CRS framework to adaptively learn representations of various levels of preference facet and diverse types of external knowledge. Extensive experiments on two publicly available benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating filter bubbles and enhancing recommendation quality in CRS.

</details>


### [22] [Reasoning Quality Matters: Combating Reasoning Collapse in LLM-based Embedding Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20563)
*Zihan Gong,Xiaohan Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFree两阶段框架，将LLM推理逐步融入查询与文档的嵌入优化，同时保持推理质量，缓解"推理坍塌"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检索嵌入方法要么将嵌入学习视为被动特征提取，要么通过指令跟随利用推理能力优化嵌入。但嵌入目标专一化会抑制有价值的推理生成或产生检索无关文本，作者将这两种退化形式称为"推理坍塌"（reasoning collapse）。

Method: CoFree分两阶段：第一阶段采用参考引导的监督微调，恢复基础嵌入模型的推理能力并保留其表示强度；第二阶段引入双奖励（面向嵌入的奖励与面向推理的奖励），通过强化学习保证对相关性进行细粒度推理。该端点耦合优化将嵌入学习从静态对齐转变为高质量推理引导的检索搜索过程。

Result: 在MTEB与BRIGHT的22个数据集上，CoFree-4B相较Qwen3-Embedding-4B平均绝对提升2.8个nDCG@10点；真实检索系统的在线实验也显示一致增益。代码、RTED与模型检查点将公开。

Conclusion: CoFree通过两阶段渐进式整合LLM推理与嵌入优化，有效缓解推理坍塌，在离线与在线检索任务中均取得显著且一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for producing context-rich text embeddings for retrieval. Most existing methods either treat embedding learning as passive feature extraction or exploit LLM reasoning through instruction following for better embedding optimization. However, specialization toward embedding objectives can suppress useful reasoning generation or produce retrieval-irrelevant text. We refer to these two forms of degradation as reasoning collapse. To address this issue, we propose CoFree (Collapse-Free Reasoning Embedding), a two-stage framework that progressively integrates LLM reasoning into query and document embedding optimization while preserving reasoning quality. At the first stage, CoFree applies reference-guided supervised fine-tuning to restore the reasoning ability and retain representational strength of the foundation embedding model. At the second stage, we introduce dual rewards, an embedding-oriented reward and a reasoning-oriented reward, to guarantee fine-grained reasoning of the relevance toward the embedding goal in reinforcement learning. This endpoint-coupled optimization transforms embedding learning from static alignment into a high-quality reasoning-guided search process for retrieval. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of CoFree, with CoFree-4B achieving an average absolute improvement of 2.8 nDCG@10 points over Qwen3-Embedding-4B across 22 datasets from MTEB and BRIGHT. Online experiments in a real-world retrieval system further show consistent gains. Code, RTED, and model checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>

### [23] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构造了ETD在二状态马尔可夫链上的反例：均值映射收缩但采样乘积具有正Lyapunov指数，并据此提出正则化emphatic TD（RETD）以修复冲击后动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD虽能稳定期望off-policy TD更新并改变其投影几何，但这两条性质均不决定常数步长的采样动力学；需要厘清"均值收缩"与"采样稳定性"之间的分离，并给出可行的修复机制。

Method: 构造遍历性二状态反例，用再生周期（regenerative-cycle）分析把Lyapunov指数的符号与follow-on迹的无限方差分离；提出RETD——一种归一化的一阶冲击后修复，保持迹与重要性比不变，将emphatic TD信号存入泄漏标量状态并释放延迟校正；其原始平衡点是ETD平衡点的仿射平移，单/双正则化读出可精确恢复ETD不动点。

Result: 证明调和递减步长下的几乎必然收敛，并在马尔可夫随机乘积界下给出条件性常数步长矩收缩结果；RETD在二状态构造与一个Baird点上获得经认证的负指数，而正Baird ETD符号仍为数值观测。配对10,000次运行实验验证了两种分离、不动点恢复、非单调稳定域与任务依赖性。

Conclusion: RETD改变了冲击后动力学，但不减少共享的follow-on迹方差，说明其改进来源于冲击后校正而非方差缩减；这进一步揭示ETD类算法的稳定性不单由均值映射的收缩性决定。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [24] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioPhys-Bridge，一个面向生物物理文献、强调证据溯源的科学推理基准数据集，包含 500 个案例与 1,517 个任务，用于评测问答与 RAG 的归因、忠实性与幻觉抑制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在跨学科科研文献分析上面临独特挑战：生物物理研究要求将观测数据锚定到原始证据、通过定量物理模型进行解释，并最终关联到生物学机制，现有基准难以覆盖这种多步、证据驱动的推理需求。

Method: 构建 BioPhys-Bridge 基准：每个案例包含证据块、稳定的证据 ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制以及后续决策，作为问答（QA）与检索增强生成（RAG）的接地目标。首个版本含 500 个案例、1,517 个面向智能体的任务，覆盖六类生物学领域与九类物理模型族（其中三个稀疏族留待后续扩展）。对所有案例执行严格的模式、证据完整性、定量接地、来源许可、去重、单位归一化等质量门控，并由领域专家对 81 个案例进行审查与标注。

Result: 初步评测显示，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 取得最高的证据 ID F1 分数（0.360），其后为 Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）与 GPT-4o-mini（0.294）。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge 是一个跨学科基准，可用于评测复杂多步科学推理中的归因能力、忠实性、幻觉抑制以及生物实验设计；未来工作将扩大数据集规模与复杂度并开展全面评测，代码与数据已在 GitHub 与 Hugging Face 开源。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [25] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统梳理2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇评估资源论文，通过分阶段筛选与自动全文编码，刻画LLM评测在目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制上的演化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型排行榜本身难以揭示"评测需求"如何变化。作者转向基准的多样性这一视角，考察研究者对LLM能力预期的转变：期望模型做什么、以及什么算作成功表现。

Method: 构建14,767篇提出或更新评测资源的arXiv论文语料（2022.01–2026.08）；采用分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，对三类维度进行结构化标注：目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制，并按时期分组比较变化趋势。

Result: 评测重心日益转向行动、交互与专业应用；既有与新兴设计要素常并存。模型参与度发展不均衡：基于LLM的评分在Agent组与非Agent组均持续增长，而模型生成材料在近期队列中无同等持续上升。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示公共研究如何将能力预期转化为具体测试与成功标准，并提出关键反思：当AI参与构造测试、执行任务并评判回答时，评测的扩张是提供了更多独立证据，还是可能复制参与模型自身的偏好与盲区。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [26] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，提出应构建"基础模型操作系统"（FMOS），通过虚拟化基础模型交互来统一管理状态、内存、预算、模型选择与策略执行，解决当前智能体框架碎片化与治理脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用已从单一单体基础模型（FM）转向复合式智能体系统，但当前技术栈高度碎片化：尽管MCP、A2A等协议改善了工具与智能体的连通性，各框架仍各自内置隐式运行时来处理状态、内存、预算与护栏，导致行为不可移植、治理脆弱，类似操作系统出现之前的计算时代——每个程序都需重复实现基础服务。因此，领域亟需一个统一系统层。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）方式论证，而非实验性方法。核心设计思想是：FMOS作为系统层，对基础模型交互进行虚拟化，类比虚拟机抽象物理硬件，使应用获得"专属、可信、能力近乎无界"的FM实例的错觉。其内部承担三项核心职能：跨内存层级的知识编排、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行。同时借鉴人脑快直觉与慢思考的切换机制，使FMOS学习何时介入、何时直接放行推理，并基于运行经验持续自适应调整策略。

Result: 作为立场论文，本文未提供定量实验结果，其"结果"为概念性主张：论证了FMOS这一系统层的必要性，并给出其应包含的抽象目标（虚拟化FM交互）、内部职能（内存编排、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行）及自适应决策机制。

Conclusion: 作者主张AI领域需要一个基础模型操作系统（FMOS），类比操作系统之于计算机、虚拟机之于物理硬件，将状态、内存、预算、模型选择与治理等能力上升为统一系统层，从而提升行为的可移植性与治理的稳健性，并通过持续学习实现介入策略的自适应。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [27] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TranSGrid 测试台，将演绎、归纳、溯因推理统一于单一任务，用于严格刻画系统性泛化；实验证明现有简化任务（近似线性动作组合、基于产出性的测试、动作显式目标）会削弱归纳或溯因需求，从而使评测退化为普通留出集测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性泛化（通过重组已知原子元素解决新问题）是人类智能的核心，但在受控条件下难以严格研究。既有研究依赖近似线性动作组合、基于产出性的测试、动作显式目标等简化，虽便于研究，却遗漏了该能力的一些本质方面。作者希望刻画这些简化究竟掩盖了什么。

Method: 以推理为中心视角，提出 TranSGrid 测试台，把演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理统一在一个任务中。在 4,800 个 TranSGrid 实例上测试七个 Transformer；并设计两个变体：其一使动作几乎线性组合（降低归纳需求），其二使目标动作显式（降低溯因需求），以对照观察求解率变化。

Result: 所有模型在 TranSGrid 上的表现远差于留出测试集：最大模型在测试集上解得 79.6%，而在 TranSGrid 上仅 55.3%，在最难子集上仅 15.8%；且该差距在训练长度范围内依然存在，说明仅凭产出性不足以评估系统性泛化。两个变体中求解率均回升至接近测试集水平，表明任一简化单独就足以把 TranSGrid 退化为普通留出测试集。

Conclusion: 现有任务至少削弱了归纳与溯因需求中的一项或两项；全面衡量系统性泛化需要一个同时涉及演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理形式的任务。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [28] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了四款主流对话式LLM平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek）的智能体式Web搜索全流程，结合真实用户交互（invivo）与同模型API受控实验（invitro），揭示了搜索调用决策、查询策略、领域偏好及结果落地等方面的差异与隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式LLM智能体日益依赖Web搜索，但其智能体式搜索的端到端生命周期仍缺乏理解，亟需系统性刻画以指导未来AI智能体与面向对话检索的搜索工具设计。

Method: 开展首个跨四大主流对话平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek）的Web搜索研究，采用双轨设计：invivo采集真实用户交互，invitro通过各平台同款模型的API进行受控实验；考察四个环节——是否调用Web搜索的智能体决策、查询构建策略、所获搜索结果的领域偏好、以及将搜索结果转化为有据可依回答时的选择。

Result: Web搜索调用决策在不同平台与模型间差异显著，且更频繁调用搜索并不必然带来更优回答质量；对话智能体采用不同的复杂查询策略；平台专属搜索引擎返回的搜索结果来自其偏好域名；回答虽大体基于搜索结果，但部分论断依赖未被引用的搜索结果，引发归属与可靠性担忧。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了对话式智能体Web搜索在全流程各环节的系统性差异与可靠性风险，对面向对话检索优化的未来AI智能体与Web搜索工具设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [29] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTuring 让同一智能体在同一 15 维加速器空间上分别以"有意义的架构旋钮"和"无意义的 [0,1] 匿名变量"两种框架执行设计任务，从而把"设计改进"与"是否真正理解机器"分离开来；两条件之差即为测得的"意义收益"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作不断报告智能体能成功设计硬件，但这类结果只能说明设计变好了，无法说明原因：智能体可能真的在对机器进行推理，也可能只是在对其从未恢复语义的"旋钮"做有效搜索。前者才能迁移到下一代架构，而后者不能。既有评测无法区分二者，因为它们改变的是智能体，却固定了问题的表述框架；本文则反过来，固定智能体、只改变问题表述。

Method: 同一智能体、同一 15 维加速器设计空间，构成两组条件：(1) 具名架构旋钮并配以模拟器计数器；(2) 在 [0,1] 上的匿名变量。两组的评估器、合法设计空间与可达最优值完全一致，唯一变量是"问题是否有意义"。在九个 kernel 的 FP16 GEMM 基准集上进行对比，每条件 5–6 次运行，单个建模加速器；另引入 critic loop（批判回路）检验意义与结构化批评之间的关系。

Result: 在有意义的条件下，"架构师"智能体平均优于建模的 H200 达 5.4%，并优于其"盲"对手 12.3%，同时模拟器调用次数减少 70.1%。但意义的收益并非独占：critic loop 能为盲智能体追回大部分差距，而对架构师智能体几乎毫无增益，说明架构知识（architectural knowledge）与结构化批评（structured critique）之间是替代关系而非互补关系。

Conclusion: 本文为初步发现（单建模加速器、每条件 5–6 次运行），其核心贡献在于"这种对照比较本身"而非所设计的加速器；它提供了一种可区分"真正理解机器"与"盲目调参"的评测范式，并揭示架构知识与结构化批评的可替代性。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [30] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架 MAGS，以 Dafny 为验证中间表示，为 LLM 生成的程序自动生成带形式化安全保证的可执行代码，在 220 个任务上实现 100% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体生成复杂程序的规模已超出人工审查能力，模糊测试、静态分析和 LLM-as-a-Verifier 等手段难以覆盖所有边界情况，存在安全与安全隐患。传统形式化验证虽能提供机器可检验的保证，但需要大量人工规格编写与证明工程，成本过高，难以规模化应用于 LLM 生成代码。

Method: 提出统一多智能体框架 MAGS，使用验证感知的中间表示 Dafny 承载安全属性并进行机器检验。流程包括：形式化并冻结经人工审计的 API 与安全需求；将生成代码翻译为 Dafny；利用验证器反馈修复违规；将验证通过的程序编译回可执行代码。核心是多智能体分工协作完成翻译、验证与修复闭环。

Result: 在 100 个 CUDA 内核、100 个终端脚本和 20 个机械臂任务共 220 个样例上评估，针对冻结规格的非平凡安全保证达到 100% 成功率。独立的安 全性与功能性评估在三个领域均表现良好，同时发现当自动形式化的语义未能完整刻画目标行为时会出现失败。

Conclusion: MAGS 表明以 Dafny 为验证中间表示的多智能体框架可在无需大量人工证明工程的前提下，为 LLM 生成程序提供机器可检验的形式化安全保证，具备跨领域可扩展性。局限在于安全保证的有效性依赖自动形式化语义与目标行为的一致性，语义建模偏差会导致验证盲区。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [31] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出工具增强LLM智能体存在"调用不存在的工具/参数"这一结构性盲区，现有工具选择与安全门控方法均无法拦截；提出五类幻觉分类法与免训练解析器Resolution Rung，并构建HTB基准，跨十个模型测得322次真实幻觉，且扩展至MCP场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御要么做工具选择，要么对真实工具调用做因果门控，二者都预设"发出的调用指向真实工具"。而幻觉调用在构造上就不是任何门控做出的决策，因此任何门控都无法拒绝它，形成结构性盲区。

Method: 主要是测量与基准研究：(1) 给出工具幻觉的五类分类法H1-H5；(2) 作为参照点提出Resolution Rung——免训练、封闭世界的解析器（注册表成员检查+签名校验），关注其应处位置而非计算内容；(3) 证明幻觉防御必须先于任何因果门控，并刻画唯一不可约残余（借用的参数在schema上与合法调用无法区分）；(4) 在两种调用界面上跨十个托管模型测量；(5) 扩展至Model Context Protocol，提出第二个分类法M1-M5；(6) 发布带版本的Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB)。

Result: 跨十个托管模型、两种调用界面共测得322次真实幻觉；捏造工具调用集中于无约束的raw-JSON界面（34 vs. 3），且模型规模无帮助（675B模型与7-8B相当）。在真实MCP界面上测得154次幻觉，包括在单注册表界面上表现干净的前沿模型也出现幻觉，因为碰撞与遮蔽是合并的结构性产物。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉属于任何门控都无法覆盖的结构性盲点，防御必须前置；单注册表解析在MCP多服务器合并命名空间下不足，需要能表达合并语义的解析机制；HTB的发布使不同解析器可跨提交比较。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [32] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将目标视为可组合的表征，类比语言学与逻辑学中的句法-语义界面，系统梳理不同目标表征的性质，并提出一个更广的设计空间框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学与计算机科学通常把目标看作施加于行为的固定约束，但鲜有研究关注目标表征本身的性质（如表达力、设计、效率）。作者希望突出"目标作为表征及其内容"这一视角，以揭示与语言学、逻辑学中句法-语义界面的平行性，并厘清关于目标的基础性问题。

Method: 概念性综述与综合。作者将目标理解为与世界知识灵活组合以组织目的性行为的认知状态，即内容关涉理性行为的可组合表征；并借鉴句法-语义界面的分析思路，识别施加于目标表征自身的约束（如某种目标语言是否有足够表达力以刻画所关注的行为、不同目标表征是否刻画同一行为），进而综合相关研究刻画不同目标表征的性质。

Result: 将不同目标表征的性质综合归纳为更广设计空间中的若干要点；揭示区分目标的"形式"与"意义"能够澄清研究中对目标的隐含假设，为高层认知与动机之间交互的研究提供启示，并分离出不同目标概念之间的变异轴。

Conclusion: 目标应被视为具有内容与形式的表征而非仅固定约束，其表达力、设计与效率等属性构成一个设计空间；区分目标的形式与意义有助于澄清隐含假设、串联认知与动机研究，并为比较不同目标理论提供分析维度。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [33] [FINSKILLOPS: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for SEC Filing QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19680)
*Yanzhang Ma,Zhenghan Tai,Hanwei Wu,Sizhe Guan,Jianliang Lei,Hailin He,Chaolong Jiang,Jijun Chi,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Bohuai Xiao,Jingrui Tian,Xinlu Wu,Xingao Zhan,Peng Lu,Muzhi Li,Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Sicheng Lyu,Tianshuo Yan,Junhao Zhu,Yaqian Xu,Lei Ding,Yufei Cui,Ziquan Liu,Boyu Han,Hengli Liu,Ling Zhou,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将金融问答系统的部署后改进重新定义为"受控行为维护"，提出FINSKILLOPS多智能体系统，通过从分型故障诊断中提炼可复用技能，并以定向验证、保护用例回归检查、负对照和版本化替换/退役进行治理，在六个金融QA基准上取得最高指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融QA系统的改进多在部署前通过检索、提示或智能体协调完成，部署后可靠性行为固定不变。而新的SEC文件问题会反复暴露期间、实体、证据使用和计算等异质错误。现有自改进方法虽能将失败转化为新行为，却难以控制纠正的应用范围，也难以避免破坏原本正确的答案。因此需要一种可控的部署后改进机制。

Method: 提出FINSKILLOPS多智能体SEC文件问答系统。其核心是将反复出现的失败转化为有范围的技能补丁：从基于证据、分类型的故障诊断中提炼可复用技能；通过定向验证、保护用例回归检查、负对照以及版本化替换或退役来治理技能；每个补丁需通过验证、不引入回归才获准部署。

Result: 在六个金融QA基准上，单一冻结的技能注册表在受评估系统中取得最高的判定加权正确率和参考一致性。进化后的技能将增强基准上的正确率从3.70提升到4.55。在另一项12轮运行研究中，33个候选技能仅6个被晋升，同时监控非纠正率从20.0%降到12.5%。

Conclusion: 受控的技能范围、准入与生命周期管理是可靠自改进的基础，为金融问答系统的部署后持续改进提供了可控且防回归的框架。

Abstract: Financial QA systems are typically improved before deployment through better retrieval, prompting, or agent coordination, leaving their reliability behavior fixed thereafter. In practice, new SEC-filing questions repeatedly expose heterogeneous errors in period, entity, evidence use, and calculation. Existing self-improvement methods can turn failures into new behaviors, but offer limited control over where a correction should apply or which previously correct answers it may break. We therefore frame post-deployment improvement as controlled behavioral maintenance: recurring failures should become scoped skill patches, and each patch should earn deployment with- out introducing regressions. We instantiate this view in FINSKILLOPS, a multi-agent system for SEC filing QA. FINSKILLOPS derives reusable skills from evidence-grounded, typed failure diagnoses and governs them through targeted validation, protected-case regression checks, negative controls, and versioned replacement or retirement. Across six financial QA benchmarks, a single frozen skill registry achieves the highest verdict-weighted correctness and reference consistency among the evaluated systems. Evolved skills raise correctness from 3.70 to 4.55 on our enhanced benchmark. In a separate 12-round operational study, only six of 33 proposed skills are promoted, while the monitoring non-correct rate falls from 20.0% to 12.5%. These results establish controlled skill scope, admission, and lifecycle management as the foundation for reliable self-improvement.

</details>


### [34] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖图框架形式化组合推理，定义三级组合复杂度，通过数据结构任务实证发现"分解→组合"不对称性：分解技能训练难以迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务，并给出理论解释及多维度泛化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据有限，模型必须通过组合已学技能来泛化。后训练方法（如强化学习）虽显著提升了语言模型推理能力，但其对组合推理的影响尚不清楚，且缺乏对组合性的形式化定义与系统评估。

Method: 提出依赖图（dependency-graph）框架形式化组合推理，划分出复杂度递增的三个组合层次；以数据结构任务为实例，因其奖励可确定性计算且组合结构清晰，用于系统实验；并进行理论分析，评估长度外推、结构分布偏移、迁移到需未见技能任务下的组合泛化；最后在真实工具调用基准上开展试点研究。

Result: 发现一致的"分解→组合"不对称性：分解技能训练不能可靠迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更容易迁移回分解任务；理论解释揭示了该不对称性的成因；在长度外推、结构分布偏移及需未见技能迁移等设定下评估了组合泛化表现。

Conclusion: 组合推理存在方向性不对称，组合训练比分解训练更具泛化优势；依赖图框架为组合性提供可操作的分层定义；工具调用基准的试点结果初步表明该不对称性可延伸至实际应用场景。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways.
